1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -uo pipefail
3	
4	# test-cross-client-sync.sh -- Verify FUSE mount picks up file edits made via SDK.
5	#
6	# Usage: ./test-cross-client-sync.sh [mount-point] [api-url]
7	#   mount-point  Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
8	#   api-url      Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
9	#
10	# Environment:
11	#   TEST_SECRET  Shared secret for test-login (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
12	#
13	# This test exercises the cross-client sync path:
14	# 1. Write a file via the FUSE mount (desktop client)
15	# 2. Edit the file via the SDK (simulating a web UI edit)
16	# 3. Wait for the FUSE mount to detect the change (IPNS polling)
17	# 4. Read the file from the FUSE mount and verify updated content
18	#
19	# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
20	
21	MP="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
22	API_URL="${2:-http://localhost:3000}"
23	SECRET="${TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]}"
24	TEST_EMAIL="dev-key@cipherbox.local"
25	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
26	REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../../.." && pwd)"
27	TEST_FILE="sync-test-${RANDOM}.txt"
28	ORIGINAL_CONTENT="Original content from FUSE mount"
29	EDITED_CONTENT="Edited content from SDK client"
30	
31	PASS=0
32	FAIL=0
33	
34	pass() {
35	  PASS=$((PASS + 1))
36	  echo "PASS: $1"
37	}
38	
39	fail() {
40	  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
41	  echo "FAIL: $1"
42	}
43	
44	ensure_runtime() {
45	  if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
46	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/api-client/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
47	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/core/dist/index.mjs" ] && \
48	     [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/packages/crypto/dist/index.mjs" ]; then
49	    return 0
50	  fi
51	
52	  echo "Building SDK dependencies..."
53	  pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/crypto build && \
54	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/core build && \
55	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/api-client build && \
56	    pnpm --dir "$REPO_ROOT" --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
57	}
58	
59	run_verify() {
60	  TEST_SECRET="$SECRET" node "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/verify-filepointer.mjs" \
61	    --api-url "$API_URL" \
62	    --email "$TEST_EMAIL" \
63	    --file-name "$TEST_FILE" \
64	    --expected-content "$1"
65	}
66	
67	run_edit() {
68	  TEST_SECRET="$SECRET" node "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs" \
69	    --api-url "$API_URL" \
70	    --email "$TEST_EMAIL" \
71	    --file-name "$TEST_FILE" \
72	    --new-content "$1"
73	}
74	
75	run_rename_folder() {
76	  TEST_SECRET="$SECRET" node "$REPO_ROOT/packages/sdk-core/scripts/rename-folder.mjs" \
77	    --api-url "$API_URL" \
78	    --email "$TEST_EMAIL" \
79	    --folder-name "$1" \
80	    --new-name "$2"
81	}
82	
83	echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Tests ==="
84	echo "Mount point: $MP"
85	echo "API URL:     $API_URL"
86	echo "Test email:  $TEST_EMAIL"
87	echo "Test file:   $TEST_FILE"
88	echo ""
89	
90	ensure_runtime || {
91	  echo "FATAL: Failed to build SDK dependencies."
92	  exit 1
93	}
94	
95	# ---- Test 1: Write file via FUSE mount ----
96	echo "--- Test 1: Write file via FUSE mount ---"
97	printf '%s' "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" > "$MP/$TEST_FILE"
98	sleep 2
99	# Trigger FUSE readdir to drain upload completions
100	ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
101	
102	FUSE_CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
103	if [ "$FUSE_CONTENT" = "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" ]; then
104	  pass "Write file via FUSE mount"
105	else
106	  fail "Write file via FUSE mount (got: '$FUSE_CONTENT')"
107	fi
108	
109	# ---- Test 2: Wait for file to be visible via SDK ----
110	echo "--- Test 2: Wait for file to be visible via SDK ---"
111	SDK_VERIFY_OK=0
112	for attempt in $(seq 1 24); do
113	  # Trigger FUSE readdir each iteration to drain upload completions
114	  stat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
115	  ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
116	  sleep 5
117	  if VERIFY_OUTPUT=$(run_verify "$ORIGINAL_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
118	    SDK_VERIFY_OK=1
119	    break
120	  fi
121	  echo "  SDK verify attempt $attempt: $VERIFY_OUTPUT"
122	done
123	
124	if [ "$SDK_VERIFY_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
125	  pass "File visible and decryptable via SDK"
126	  echo "  $VERIFY_OUTPUT"
127	else
128	  fail "File not visible via SDK after 120s"
129	  echo "FATAL: Cannot proceed without SDK-visible file."
130	  echo ""
131	  echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
132	  echo "  Passed: $PASS"
133	  echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
134	  echo "=================================="
135	  # Cleanup
136	  rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
137	  exit "$FAIL"
138	fi
139	
140	# ---- Test 3: Edit file via SDK ----
141	echo "--- Test 3: Edit file via SDK ---"
142	if EDIT_OUTPUT=$(run_edit "$EDITED_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
143	  pass "Edit file via SDK"
144	  echo "  $EDIT_OUTPUT"
145	else
146	  fail "Edit file via SDK ($EDIT_OUTPUT)"
147	  echo "FATAL: Cannot test sync without successful edit."
148	  echo ""
149	  echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
150	  echo "  Passed: $PASS"
151	  echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
152	  echo "=================================="
153	  # Cleanup
154	  rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
155	  exit "$FAIL"
156	fi
157	
158	# ---- Test 4: Verify edit persisted via SDK ----
159	echo "--- Test 4: Verify edit persisted via SDK ---"
160	sleep 2
161	if VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT=$(run_verify "$EDITED_CONTENT" 2>&1); then
162	  pass "Edited content verified via SDK"
163	  echo "  $VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT"
164	else
165	  fail "Edited content not verified via SDK ($VERIFY_EDIT_OUTPUT)"
166	fi
167	
168	# ---- Test 5: Wait for FUSE mount to pick up edited content ----
169	echo "--- Test 5: Wait for FUSE mount to detect edit ---"
170	# The FUSE mount polls folder metadata every 30s. After detecting a newer
171	# modified_at on the FilePointer, it re-resolves the file's IPNS record
172	# to pick up the new CID. Allow up to 120s for two full polling cycles.
173	SYNC_OK=0
174	for attempt in $(seq 1 24); do
175	  sleep 5
176	  # Trigger readdir to reliably drain refresh completions via
177	  # drain_refresh_completions() -> populate_folder(), which detects
178	  # modified_at changes and marks files for re-resolution. We use `ls`
179	  # here because it is a dependable refresh trigger during polling.
180	  ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
181	  stat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
182	  FUSE_CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
183	  if [ "$FUSE_CONTENT" = "$EDITED_CONTENT" ]; then
184	    SYNC_OK=1
185	    echo "  Sync detected on attempt $attempt ($(( attempt * 5 ))s)"
186	    break
187	  fi
188	  echo "  Sync poll attempt $attempt: content still original"
189	done
190	
191	if [ "$SYNC_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
192	  pass "FUSE mount picked up edited content"
193	else
194	  fail "FUSE mount still shows original content after 120s (got: '$FUSE_CONTENT')"
195	fi
196	
197	# ---- Test 6: Rename folder via SDK ----
198	echo "--- Test 6: Rename folder via SDK ---"
199	
200	# 6a. Create a folder with a file inside via FUSE
201	RENAME_DIR="sync-rename-test"
202	RENAMED_DIR="sync-renamed-folder"
203	RENAME_INNER_CONTENT="rename test content"
204	
205	mkdir -p "$MP/$RENAME_DIR"
206	printf '%s' "$RENAME_INNER_CONTENT" > "$MP/$RENAME_DIR/inner.txt"
207	sleep 3
208	# Trigger readdir to flush uploads
209	ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
210	ls "$MP/$RENAME_DIR" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
211	
212	# 6b. Wait for the FUSE mount's metadata publish to complete and propagate.
213	# The folder is immediately visible locally, but the IPNS publish takes
214	# 1.5s debounce + publish time. We must wait for this to finish before the
215	# SDK rename, otherwise the FUSE mount's pending publish can overwrite the
216	# SDK's renamed metadata. Verify via SDK rename success (not local stat).
217	RENAME_READY=0
218	RENAME_OK=0
219	echo "  Waiting for FUSE metadata publish to propagate before SDK rename..."
220	for rename_attempt in $(seq 1 12); do
221	  sleep 10
222	  # Trigger readdir to drain publish completions
223	  ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
224	  if RENAME_OUTPUT=$(run_rename_folder "$RENAME_DIR" "$RENAMED_DIR" 2>&1); then
225	    RENAME_OK=1
226	    echo "  SDK rename succeeded on attempt $rename_attempt ($(( rename_attempt * 10 ))s)"
227	    break
228	  fi
229	  echo "  SDK rename attempt $rename_attempt: folder not yet in remote metadata"
230	done
231	
232	if [ "$RENAME_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
233	  pass "Rename folder via SDK"
234	  echo "  $RENAME_OUTPUT"
235	  RENAME_READY=1
236	else
237	  fail "Rename folder via SDK (not visible in remote metadata after 120s: $RENAME_OUTPUT)"
238	  echo "FATAL: Cannot test folder rename sync without successful rename."
239	fi
240	
241	# ---- Test 7: FUSE mount detects folder rename ----
242	echo "--- Test 7: Wait for FUSE mount to detect folder rename ---"
243	if [ "$RENAME_READY" -ne 1 ]; then
244	  fail "Skipped: folder rename prerequisite failed"
245	else
246	  # The FUSE mount polls folder metadata every 30s. After detecting the rename
247	  # (child entry name changed + modifiedAt bumped), it should show the new
248	  # folder name and remove the old one. On macOS with FUSE-T's SMB backend,
249	  # mutated_folders blocking (30s) + cache TTL resets + SMB directory caching
250	  # can compound delays. Allow up to 300s and treat timeout as optional.
251	  RENAME_SYNC_OK=0
252	  for attempt in $(seq 1 60); do
253	    sleep 5
254	    # Trigger readdir to drain refresh completions
255	    ls "$MP" > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
256	    if [ -d "$MP/$RENAMED_DIR" ] && [ ! -d "$MP/$RENAME_DIR" ]; then
257	      RENAME_SYNC_OK=1
258	      echo "  Rename sync detected on attempt $attempt ($(( attempt * 5 ))s)"
259	      break
260	    fi
261	    if [ $(( attempt % 10 )) -eq 0 ]; then
262	      echo "  Rename sync poll attempt $attempt: not yet renamed"
263	    fi
264	  done
265	
266	  if [ "$RENAME_SYNC_OK" -eq 1 ]; then
267	    # Verify nested content survived the rename
268	    INNER_CONTENT=$(cat "$MP/$RENAMED_DIR/inner.txt" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
269	    if [ "$INNER_CONTENT" = "$RENAME_INNER_CONTENT" ]; then
270	      pass "FUSE mount picked up folder rename with intact content"
271	    else
272	      fail "FUSE mount renamed folder but inner content mismatch (got: '$INNER_CONTENT')"
273	    fi
274	  else
275	    if [ "$(uname -s)" = "Darwin" ]; then
276	      # Optional on macOS only: FUSE-T SMB cache timing can delay visibility
277	      # beyond 300s. The rename logic is independently verified by unit tests
278	      # and FUSE operations tests.
279	      echo "WARN: FUSE mount did not detect folder rename after 300s on macOS (optional)"
280	      echo "  This does not indicate a bug in rename logic -- see unit tests."
281	      pass "FUSE folder rename sync (optional on macOS -- timed out, see warning above)"
282	    else
283	      fail "FUSE mount did not detect folder rename after 300s"
284	    fi
285	  fi
286	fi
287	
288	# Cleanup renamed folder
289	: "${MP:?Mount point is required}"
290	rm -rf "$MP/$RENAMED_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
291	rm -rf "$MP/$RENAME_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
292	
293	# ---- Cleanup ----
294	echo "--- Cleanup ---"
295	rm -f "$MP/$TEST_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
296	sleep 2
297	
298	# ---- Summary ----
299	echo ""
300	echo "=== Cross-Client Sync Results ==="
301	echo "  Passed: $PASS"
302	echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
303	echo "=================================="
304	
305	exit "$FAIL"
306

1	/**
2	 * edit-filepointer.mjs -- Edit a file's content via the SDK.
3	 *
4	 * Authenticates via test-login, finds the target file in the vault,
5	 * encrypts new content, uploads to IPFS, updates the file's IPNS
6	 * metadata record, and republishes folder metadata with an updated
7	 * modified_at timestamp so other clients (e.g. FUSE mount) detect
8	 * the change.
9	 *
10	 * Usage:
11	 *   TEST_SECRET=<secret> node edit-filepointer.mjs \
12	 *     --api-url http://localhost:3000 \
13	 *     --email dev-key@cipherbox.local \
14	 *     --file-name hello.txt \
15	 *     --new-content "Updated content from SDK"
16	 */
17	
18	import { createAxiosInstance } from '../../api-client/dist/index.mjs';
19	import {
20	  loadVaultKeyBlob,
21	  loadFolderMetadata,
22	  resolveFileMetadata,
23	  updateFileMetadata,
24	  replaceFileInFolder,
25	  updateFolderMetadataAndPublish,
26	} from '../dist/index.mjs';
27	import {
28	  encryptAesGcm,
29	  generateFileKey,
30	  generateIv,
31	  wrapKey,
32	  unwrapKey,
33	  bytesToHex,
34	  hexToBytes,
35	  deriveVaultIpnsKeypair,
36	  clearBytes,
37	} from '../../crypto/dist/index.mjs';
38	import { addToIpfs } from '../dist/index.mjs';
39	
40	function parseArgs(argv) {
41	  const values = new Map();
42	
43	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 1) {
44	    const token = argv[i];
45	    if (!token.startsWith('--')) {
46	      throw new Error(`Unexpected argument: ${token}`);
47	    }
48	
49	    const key = token.slice(2);
50	    const value = argv[i + 1];
51	    if (value === undefined || value.startsWith('--')) {
52	      throw new Error(`Missing value for --${key}`);
53	    }
54	
55	    values.set(key, value);
56	    i += 1;
57	  }
58	
59	  if (values.has('secret')) {
60	    throw new Error('Do not pass --secret on CLI. Set TEST_SECRET in environment.');
61	  }
62	
63	  const apiUrl = values.get('api-url');
64	  const secret = process.env.TEST_SECRET;
65	  const email = values.get('email');
66	  const fileName = values.get('file-name');
67	  const newContent = values.get('new-content');
68	
69	  if (!apiUrl || !email || !fileName || !secret || newContent === undefined) {
70	    throw new Error(
71	      'Usage: edit-filepointer.mjs --api-url <url> --email <email> --file-name <name> --new-content <text> (requires TEST_SECRET env var)'
72	    );
73	  }
74	
75	  return { apiUrl, secret, email, fileName, newContent };
76	}
77	
78	async function authenticate(apiUrl, email, secret) {
79	  const response = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/auth/test-login`, {
80	    method: 'POST',
81	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
82	    body: JSON.stringify({ email, secret }),
83	  });
84	
85	  if (!response.ok) {
86	    const body = await response.text();
87	    throw new Error(`test-login failed (${response.status}): ${body}`);
88	  }
89	
90	  const payload = await response.json();
91	
92	  if (!payload.accessToken || !payload.privateKeyHex || !payload.publicKeyHex) {
93	    throw new Error('test-login response missing accessToken, privateKeyHex, or publicKeyHex');
94	  }
95	
96	  return payload;
97	}
98	
99	async function main() {
100	  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
101	  const auth = await authenticate(args.apiUrl, args.email, args.secret);
102	  const accessToken = auth.accessToken;
103	  const userPrivateKey = Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(auth.privateKeyHex, 'hex'));
104	  const userPublicKey = Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(auth.publicKeyHex, 'hex'));
105	
106	  const axiosInstance = createAxiosInstance({
107	    baseUrl: args.apiUrl,
108	    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
109	  });
110	
111	  const ctx = {
112	    apiUrl: args.apiUrl,
113	    getAccessToken: async () => accessToken,
114	    axiosInstance,
115	  };
116	
117	  // 1. Load vault key blob to get rootFolderKey
118	  const vaultKeyBlob = await loadVaultKeyBlob({ userPrivateKey, ctx });
119	  if (!vaultKeyBlob) {
120	    throw new Error('Vault key blob not found');
121	  }
122	
123	  // 2. Get vault info for rootIpnsName
124	  const vaultResponse = await axiosInstance.get('/vault');
125	  const rootIpnsName = vaultResponse.data.rootIpnsName;
126	  if (!rootIpnsName) {
127	    throw new Error('Vault response missing rootIpnsName');
128	  }
129	
130	  // 3. Load root folder metadata
131	  const folder = await loadFolderMetadata({
132	    ipnsName: rootIpnsName,
133	    folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
134	    ctx,
135	  });
136	  if (!folder) {
137	    throw new Error(`Root folder metadata not found for ${rootIpnsName}`);
138	  }
139	
140	  // 4. Find the target file's FilePointer
141	  const filePointer = folder.metadata.children.find(
142	    (child) => child.type === 'file' && child.name === args.fileName
143	  );
144	  if (!filePointer) {
145	    throw new Error(`FilePointer not found for ${args.fileName}`);
146	  }
147	  if (!filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName) {
148	    throw new Error(`FilePointer for ${args.fileName} is missing fileMetaIpnsName`);
149	  }
150	
151	  // 5. Resolve current file metadata
152	  const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
153	    filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
154	    vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
155	    ctx
156	  );
157	
158	  // 6. Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer
159	  if (!filePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
160	    throw new Error('FilePointer missing ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted (legacy files not supported)');
161	  }
162	  const fileIpnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(
163	    hexToBytes(filePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),
164	    userPrivateKey
165	  );
166	
167	  // 7. Encrypt new content
168	  const plaintext = new TextEncoder().encode(args.newContent);
169	  const fileKey = generateFileKey();
170	  const iv = generateIv();
171	  let newCid;
172	  let wrappedKeyHex;
173	  let ivHex;
174	
175	  try {
176	    const ciphertext = await encryptAesGcm(plaintext, fileKey, iv);
177	    const wrappedKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, userPublicKey);
178	    wrappedKeyHex = bytesToHex(wrappedKey);
179	    ivHex = bytesToHex(iv);
180	
181	    // 8. Upload encrypted content to IPFS
182	    const uploadResult = await addToIpfs(ctx, ciphertext);
183	    newCid = uploadResult.cid;
184	  } finally {
185	    clearBytes(fileKey);
186	  }
187	
188	  // 9. Update file metadata IPNS record
189	  let ipnsRecord;
190	  try {
191	    ({ ipnsRecord } = await updateFileMetadata({
192	      fileIpnsPrivateKey,
193	      fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
194	      folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
195	      currentMetadata,
196	      updates: {
197	        cid: newCid,
198	        fileKeyEncrypted: wrappedKeyHex,
199	        fileIv: ivHex,
200	        size: plaintext.length,
201	        encryptionMode: 'GCM',
202	      },
203	      createVersion: true,
204	      ctx,
205	    }));
206	  } finally {
207	    fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
208	  }
209	
210	  // 10. Publish only the file IPNS record
211	  await replaceFileInFolder({
212	    children: folder.metadata.children,
213	    fileId: filePointer.id,
214	    fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
215	    ctx,
216	  });
217	
218	  // 11. Republish folder metadata with updated modified_at so other clients
219	  // (e.g. FUSE mount) detect the change via the FilePointer timestamp.
220	  const updatedChildren = folder.metadata.children.map((child) => {
221	    if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === filePointer.id) {
222	      return { ...child, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
223	    }
224	    return child;
225	  });
226	
227	  // Derive root folder IPNS keypair for republishing
228	  const rootIpnsKeypair = await deriveVaultIpnsKeypair(userPrivateKey);
229	
230	  const { newSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
231	    children: updatedChildren,
232	    folderKey: vaultKeyBlob.rootFolderKey,
233	    ipnsPrivateKey: rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
234	    ipnsName: rootIpnsName,
235	    sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
236	    ctx,
237	  });
238	
239	  // Zero sensitive key material
240	  rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
241	
242	  console.log(
243	    JSON.stringify({
244	      fileName: args.fileName,
245	      oldCid: currentMetadata.cid,
246	      newCid,
247	      newContentLength: plaintext.length,
248	      folderSequenceNumber: newSequenceNumber.toString(),
249	      fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
250	    })
251	  );
252	}
253	
254	main().catch((error) => {
255	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
256	  console.error(message);
257	  process.exit(1);
258	});
259

39	export async function createAndPublishIpnsRecord(params: {
40	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
41	  ipnsPublicKey?: Uint8Array;
42	  ipnsName: string;
43	  metadataCid: string;
44	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
45	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
46	  keyEpoch?: number;
47	  expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
48	  ctx?: SdkContext;
49	}): Promise<{ success: boolean; sequenceNumber: bigint }> {
50	  return withPerf('ipns:publish', async () => {
51	    // 1. Create IPNS record pointing to /ipfs/{metadataCid}
52	    // 24 hour lifetime (will be republished by TEE every 3 hours)
53	    const record = await createIpnsRecord(
54	      params.ipnsPrivateKey,
55	      `/ipfs/${params.metadataCid}`,
56	      params.sequenceNumber,
57	      24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 24 hours in ms
58	    );
59	
60	    // 2. Marshal to bytes for transport
61	    const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
62	    const publicKeyBytes = params.ipnsPublicKey ?? deriveEd25519PublicKey(params.ipnsPrivateKey);
63	
64	    // 3. Base64 encode for API transmission (loop-based to avoid call stack overflow on large records)
65	    let binary = '';
66	    for (let i = 0; i < recordBytes.length; i++) {
67	      binary += String.fromCharCode(recordBytes[i]);
68	    }
69	    const recordBase64 = btoa(binary);
70	    binary = '';
71	    for (let i = 0; i < publicKeyBytes.length; i++) {
72	      binary += String.fromCharCode(publicKeyBytes[i]);
73	    }
74	    const publicKey = btoa(binary);
75	
76	    // 4. Call backend API to relay to IPFS network
77	    const apiOptions = params.ctx?.axiosInstance
78	      ? { _axiosInstance: params.ctx.axiosInstance }
79	      : undefined;
80	    const response = await ipnsControllerPublishRecord(
81	      {
82	        ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
83	        record: recordBase64,
84	        publicKey,
85	        metadataCid: params.metadataCid,
86	        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
87	        keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
88	        expectedSequenceNumber: params.expectedSequenceNumber,
89	      },
90	      apiOptions
91	    );
92	
93	    return {
94	      success: response.success,
95	      sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
96	    };
97	  });
98	}
99

104	 * which processes them with concurrency-limited parallelism.
105	 * Partial success is allowed: individual failures do not fail the batch.

107	 * @param records - Array of IPNS record payloads to publish

225	export async function updateFileMetadata(params: {
226	  fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
227	  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
228	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
229	  currentMetadata: FileMetadata;
230	  updates: Partial<
231	    Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>
232	  >;
233	  createVersion: boolean;
234	  maxVersionsPerFile?: number;
235	  ctx: SdkContext;
236	}): Promise<{
237	  ipnsName: string;
238	  metadataCid: string;
239	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
240	  prunedCids: string[];
241	}> {
242	  const maxVersions = params.maxVersionsPerFile ?? MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE;
243	
244	  // 1. Build version history for the initial (pre-conflict) metadata
245	  let versions: VersionEntry[] | undefined;
246	  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
247	
248	  if (params.createVersion) {
249	    const versionEntry: VersionEntry = {
250	      cid: params.currentMetadata.cid,
251	      fileKeyEncrypted: params.currentMetadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
252	      fileIv: params.currentMetadata.fileIv,
253	      size: params.currentMetadata.size,
254	      timestamp: Date.now(),
255	      encryptionMode: params.currentMetadata.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
256	    };
257	    const allVersions = [versionEntry, ...(params.currentMetadata.versions ?? [])];
258	
259	    versions = allVersions.slice(0, maxVersions);
260	    prunedCids = allVersions.slice(maxVersions).map((v) => v.cid);
261	  } else {
262	    versions = params.currentMetadata.versions;
263	  }
264	
265	  // 2. Merge updates into current metadata
266	  const updatedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
267	    ...params.currentMetadata,
268	    ...params.updates,
269	    ...(versions && versions.length > 0 ? { versions } : { versions: undefined }),
270	    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
271	  };
272	
273	  // 3+4. Resolve the current IPNS record (CAS base) and encrypt+upload the updated
274	  //      metadata concurrently — they are independent (the uploaded CID does not
275	  //      depend on the resolved sequence number), saving one serial round trip.
276	  const [resolved, uploadedCid] = await Promise.all([
277	    resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx),
278	    encryptAndUpload(updatedMetadata, params.folderKey, params.ctx),
279	  ]);
280	  if (!resolved) {
281	    throw new Error(
282	      `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
283	    );
284	  }
285	  let currentSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
286	  let currentCid = uploadedCid;
287	
288	  // 5. Publish with CAS — on 409 apply latest-wins + loser-becomes-version, then retry once
289	  try {
290	    // Attempt 1
291	    try {
292	      const result = await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
293	        ipnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
294	        ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
295	        metadataCid: currentCid,
296	        sequenceNumber: currentSeq + 1n,
297	        expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
298	        ctx: params.ctx,
299	      });
300	      return {
301	        ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
302	        metadataCid: currentCid,
303	        newSequenceNumber: result.sequenceNumber,
304	        prunedCids,
305	      };
306	    } catch (err) {
307	      if (!is409(err)) throw err; // Non-409: propagate unchanged
308	
309	      // --- Conflict merge ---
310	      // Re-resolve authoritatively
311	      const reResolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx);
312	      if (!reResolved) {
313	        throw new ConflictError(params.fileMetaIpnsName, 1, currentSeq);
314	      }
315	      const lastRemoteSeq = reResolved.sequenceNumber;
316	      currentSeq = lastRemoteSeq;
317	
318	      // Fetch and decrypt remote FileMetadata
319	      const remoteMeta = await fetchAndDecryptFileMetadata(
320	        reResolved.cid,
321	        params.folderKey,
322	        params.ctx
323	      );
324	
325	      // Latest-wins by modifiedAt (>= prefers local on tie)
326	      const localModifiedAt = updatedMetadata.modifiedAt ?? 0;
327	      const remoteModifiedAt = remoteMeta.modifiedAt ?? 0;
328	      const localWins = localModifiedAt >= remoteModifiedAt;
329	
330	      const winner = localWins ? updatedMetadata : remoteMeta;
331	      const loser = localWins ? remoteMeta : updatedMetadata;
332	
333	      // The loser's current content becomes a VersionEntry
334	      const loserAsVersion: VersionEntry = {
335	        cid: loser.cid,
336	        fileKeyEncrypted: loser.fileKeyEncrypted,
337	        fileIv: loser.fileIv,
338	        size: loser.size,
339	        timestamp: loser.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
340	        encryptionMode: (loser.encryptionMode as 'GCM' | 'CTR') ?? 'GCM',
341	      };
342	
343	      // Merge: winner's versions + loserAsVersion merged with the loser's version history.
344	      // The second arg MUST be loser.versions, not remoteMeta.versions: when the remote wins
345	      // (localWins === false) the loser is the LOCAL metadata, so passing remoteMeta.versions
346	      // would silently drop the local writer's prior version history.
347	      const { versions: mergedVersions, prunedCids: extraPruned } = mergeVersions(
348	        [...(winner.versions ?? []), loserAsVersion],
349	        loser.versions,
350	        maxVersions
351	      );
352	
353	      // Build merged metadata
354	      const mergedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
355	        ...winner,
356	        versions: mergedVersions.length > 0 ? mergedVersions : undefined,
357	        modifiedAt: winner.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
358	      };
359	
360	      // Filter accumulated prunedCids against the set of CIDs actually referenced by the
361	      // published mergedMetadata (CR-02 / D-07): a CID resurrected into mergedMetadata.versions
362	      // by the remote merge must NOT be returned for unpinning.  De-dupe the combined set first
363	      // to prevent phantom unpin retries from duplicate entries.
364	      const referenced = new Set([
365	        mergedMetadata.cid,
366	        ...(mergedMetadata.versions ?? []).map((v) => v.cid),
367	      ]);
368	      prunedCids = [...new Set([...prunedCids, ...extraPruned])].filter((c) => !referenced.has(c));
369	
370	      // Re-encrypt and re-upload merged metadata
371	      currentCid = await encryptAndUpload(mergedMetadata, params.folderKey, params.ctx);
372	
373	      // Attempt 2 (retry)
374	      try {
375	        const retryResult = await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
376	          ipnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
377	          ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
378	          metadataCid: currentCid,
379	          sequenceNumber: currentSeq + 1n,
380	          expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
381	          ctx: params.ctx,
382	        });
383	        return {
384	          ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
385	          metadataCid: currentCid,
386	          newSequenceNumber: retryResult.sequenceNumber,
387	          prunedCids,
388	        };
389	      } catch (retryErr) {
390	        if (is409(retryErr)) {
391	          throw new ConflictError(params.fileMetaIpnsName, 2, currentSeq);
392	        }
393	        throw retryErr;
394	      }
395	    }
396	  } finally {
397	    // Zeroize the private key on all exit paths (T-44-12 / PATTERNS shared pattern).
398	    // Caller passes the key buffer; fill(0) zeroes it in-place after publish completes.
399	    params.fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
```

So `ipnsRecord` is `undefined`, then passed to `replaceFileInFolder`. Let me confirm what `replaceFileInFolder` does with that and whether it still exists.

> AGENT

Root cause confirmed. The chain:

1. `edit-filepointer.mjs` step 9 destructures `{ ipnsRecord }` from `updateFileMetadata`.
2. But #488 changed `updateFileMetadata` to **publish the file record internally** and return `{ ipnsName, metadataCid, newSequenceNumber, prunedCids }` — no `ipnsRecord`. So `ipnsRecord` is `undefined`.
3. Step 10 calls `replaceFileInFolder({ fileIpnsRecord: undefined })`, which spreads `undefined` into a batch-publish entry → `{ ipnsName: undefined, record: undefined, metadataCid: undefined, recordType: 'file' }`.
4. The API's `PublishIpnsEntryDto` requires those fields (`@IsNotEmpty`), so the ValidationPipe rejects with **400** — exactly the deterministic, instant failure we see. (Step 9's internal publish succeeded — that's the "published successfully for k51q...ralw" in the api.log.)

The test helper script wasn't updated for the #488 contract change. Let me confirm how the **product code** now uses `updateFileMetadata` so my fix mirrors the correct pattern.

186	export async function updateFolderMetadataAndPublish(params: {
187	  children: FolderChild[];
188	  baseChildren?: FolderChild[];
189	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
190	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
191	  ipnsPublicKey?: Uint8Array;
192	  ipnsName: string;
193	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
194	  ctx: SdkContext;
195	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
196	  keyEpoch?: number;
197	}): Promise<{ cid: string; newSequenceNumber: bigint; publishedChildren: FolderChild[] }> {
198	  return withPerf('folder:update-publish', async () => {
199	    // Merge-and-republish retry loop (D-01 through D-05).
200	    // encrypt+upload happens inside the loop so each attempt gets a fresh CID (D-03).
201	    let currentSeq = params.sequenceNumber;
202	    let currentLocalChildren: FolderChild[] = params.children;
203	    let lastRemoteSeq: bigint = params.sequenceNumber;
204	
205	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt++) {
206	      // 1. Build v2 metadata with current children, encrypt, upload → fresh CID (D-03)
207	      const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
208	        version: 'v2',
209	        children: currentLocalChildren,
210	      };
211	      const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
212	      const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
213	      const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
214	      const { cid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes);
215	
216	      // 2. CAS publish: expectedSequenceNumber guards against lost update
217	      const newSeq = currentSeq + 1n;
218	      try {
219	        await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
220	          ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
221	          ipnsPublicKey: params.ipnsPublicKey,
222	          ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
223	          metadataCid: cid,
224	          sequenceNumber: newSeq,
225	          encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
226	          keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
227	          expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
228	          ctx: params.ctx,
229	        });
230	        return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq, publishedChildren: currentLocalChildren };
231	      } catch (err) {
232	        if (!is409(err)) throw err;
233	
234	        // 3. Re-resolve authoritatively — ignore any seq hint in the error body (Pitfall 1+2)
235	        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.ipnsName, params.ctx);
236	        if (!resolved) {
237	          throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, attempt + 1, lastRemoteSeq);
238	        }
239	        currentSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
240	        lastRemoteSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
241	
242	        // 4. Re-fetch + decrypt remote folder metadata
243	        const remote = await fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, params.folderKey, params.ctx);
244	
245	        // 5. Three-way merge (D-01 / D-02)
246	        if (params.baseChildren !== undefined) {
247	          // D-01: caller provided base snapshot → proper three-way merge
248	          currentLocalChildren = mergeChildren(
249	            params.baseChildren,
250	            currentLocalChildren,
251	            remote.children
252	          );
253	        } else {
254	          // D-02: no base → union fallback; log warning so the caller sweep is noticeable
255	          console.warn(
256	            '[sdk-core] updateFolderMetadataAndPublish: baseChildren not provided for ' +
257	              params.ipnsName +
258	              ' — using union fallback (deletes may resurrect). Caller should pass baseChildren.'
259	          );
260	          currentLocalChildren = mergeChildren([], currentLocalChildren, remote.children);
261	        }
262	
263	        // 6. After the final attempt, throw ConflictError (D-05)
264	        if (attempt === 3) {
265	          throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, 4, lastRemoteSeq);
266	        }
267	
268	        // 7. Backoff + jitter before next attempt (D-04)
269	        await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, retryDelayMs(attempt)));
270	      }
271	    }
272	
273	    // Unreachable fallback (TypeScript exhaustion — ConflictError thrown inside the loop above)
274	    throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, 4, lastRemoteSeq);
275	  });
276	}

## Root cause

The E2E failure is **not** from the phase-46 PR (#492 was docs-only — ROADMAP/STATE + a moved todo file, zero code). It's a latent contract drift from **#488** (`feat(sdk-core): handle IPNS write conflicts via 409 merge and file CAS publish`) that only surfaces on `main`'s E2E job:

- #488 changed `updateFileMetadata` to **publish the file IPNS record internally** and return `{ ipnsName, metadataCid, newSequenceNumber, prunedCids }` — it no longer returns an `ipnsRecord`.
- The test helper `edit-filepointer.mjs` was never updated. It still destructured `{ ipnsRecord }` (now `undefined`) and passed it to `replaceFileInFolder`, which spread `undefined` into a batch-publish entry → `{ ipnsName: undefined, record: undefined, metadataCid: undefined }`.
- The API's `PublishIpnsEntryDto` marks those `@IsNotEmpty`, and `main.ts` runs the ValidationPipe with `forbidNonWhitelisted: true` → **deterministic 400**, identical on all three platforms (Linux/macOS/Windows logs confirm `Request failed with status code 400` in ~0.5s).

The fix removes the broken redundant `replaceFileInFolder` call (the file record is already published inside `updateFileMetadata`) and adds `baseChildren` to the folder republish — exactly how the production edit path does it.

Let me verify the all-platforms-same-cause claim is fully captured and that the SDK dist actually exposes the contract I'm relying on, then I'll branch + commit + PR.

It destructured `{ ipnsRecord }` from `updateFileMetadata` → now `undefined`.\n2. Passed it to `replaceFileInFolder`, which spread `undefined` into a batch-publish entry → `{ ipnsName: undefined, record: undefined, metadataCid: undefined, recordType: 'file' }`.\n3. The API's `PublishIpnsEntryDto` marks those fields `@IsNotEmpty`, and `main.ts` runs the `ValidationPipe` with `forbidNonWhitelisted: true` → **deterministic 400** in ~0.5s, identical on every platform.\n\nThe file's own per-file record publish (step 9, inside `updateFileMetadata`) actually *succeeds* in the API logs — confirming the failure is the redundant follow-up `replaceFileInFolder` call.\n\n## Fix\n\nBring the helper in line with the production edit path in `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts`:\n\n- Stop destructuring the non-existent `ipnsRecord`; the file record is already published inside `updateFileMetadata`.\n- Delete the redundant (and now broken) `replaceFileInFolder` call + its unused import.\n- Pass `baseChildren` to `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` for a proper three-way merge base.\n\n## Verification\n\n- All 3 platform job logs confirm the same deterministic 400 at the edit step.\n- `replaceFileInFolder` remains exported (still used by the version-*restore* path via `restoreVersion`, which does return an `ipnsRecord`).\n- `node --check` passes; no residual references to removed symbols.\n- The built `sdk-core` `dist` `.d.ts` documents the replaced return shape, so the E2E (which imports from `dist`) exercises the new contract.\n\n> Note: the full desktop E2E (Kubo + Postgres + Redis + FUSE mount) was not run locally; CI re-run will confirm. The fix is a 1:1 mirror of the verified production edit path.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\nenv -u GITHUB_TOKEN gh pr create --repo FSM1/cipher-box --base main --head fix/cross-client-sync-edit-filepointer-contract --title \"fix(test): align edit-filepointer helper with updateFileMetadata internal-publish contract\" --body-file /tmp/pr-body.md 2>&1 | tail -8",

{

  "content": "---\nname: project-sdk-helper-scripts-untyped-drift\ndescription: sdk-core/scripts/*.mjs E2E helpers aren't typechecked/linted and silently drift from SDK contract changes\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\nThe `.mjs` helper scripts in `packages/sdk-core/scripts/` (edit-filepointer, verify-filepointer, rename-folder) are imported by the desktop cross-client-sync E2E (`tests/desktop-e2e/scripts/test-cross-client-sync.sh`). They import from the built `dist/` and are **not** covered by tsc, eslint (lint-staged only matches `.ts/.tsx`), or unit tests — so they silently drift when an SDK public-API contract changes and only break in the desktop E2E job on `main`.\n\n**Why:** PR #488 changed `updateFileMetadata` to publish the file IPNS record internally and return `{ ipnsName, metadataCid, newSequenceNumber, prunedCids }` (no more `ipnsRecord`). `edit-filepointer.mjs` still destructured `{ ipnsRecord }` (→ undefined) and passed it to `replaceFileInFolder`, producing a batch-publish entry with empty `ipnsName/record/metadataCid` → API `PublishIpnsEntryDto` rejected it with a deterministic 400 (ValidationPipe `forbidNonWhitelisted`), failing `test-cross-client-sync` on all platforms. Fixed in PR #495.\n\n**How to apply:** After any sdk-core public-API change, `grep packages/sdk-core/scripts/*.mjs` for the changed symbols and reconcile them against the production usage (the edit path mirrors `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts`). Relates to [[project-cross-package-dist-staleness]] (scripts read dist, so rebuild first) and [[project-web-sdk-folder-state-desync]].\n"
}

> AGENT

Now the full picture is clear, and there's a **systemic gap** worth flagging:

1. `ci-e2e.yml` has **no `pull_request` trigger** — it only runs on push to `main` or manual dispatch. So neither #488 nor this PR get E2E validation *before* merge.
2. The `desktop` path filter includes `packages/crypto/src/**` but **not `packages/sdk-core/**` or `tests/desktop-e2e/**`** — so SDK contract changes like #488 never trigger Desktop E2E even on push to main. That's how the drift reached `main`.

Since the PR won't auto-run the E2E, let me validate the fix directly by dispatching the workflow against the fix branch (manual dispatch forces both web + desktop on).

> AGENT

I've diagnosed, fixed, and shipped the fix, and a validation run is now in flight. Here's the summary while we wait for CI.

## What was wrong

The E2E failure was **not** caused by the phase-46 PR you merged — #492 was docs-only (ROADMAP/STATE + a moved todo, zero code). It was **latent contract drift from #488** that only surfaces on `main`.

- #488 (`file CAS publish`) changed `updateFileMetadata` to publish the file IPNS record **internally** and return `{ ipnsName, metadataCid, newSequenceNumber, prunedCids }` — it no longer returns `ipnsRecord`.
- The E2E helper `packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs` was never updated. It still destructured `{ ipnsRecord }` (→ `undefined`) and passed it to `replaceFileInFolder`, producing a batch-publish entry with empty `ipnsName`/`record`/`metadataCid`.
- The API's `PublishIpnsEntryDto` marks those `@IsNotEmpty` and the ValidationPipe runs `forbidNonWhitelisted` → a **deterministic 400** in ~0.5s on all three platforms (Linux/macOS/Windows logs all confirm this exact failure at *Test 3: Edit file via SDK*).

## The fix (PR #495)

`packages/sdk-core/scripts/edit-filepointer.mjs` — dropped the broken redundant `replaceFileInFolder` call (the record is already published inside `updateFileMetadata`) and added `baseChildren` to the folder republish, mirroring the production edit path in `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` 1:1. `replaceFileInFolder` stays exported (still used by the version-*restore* path).

→ **PR: https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/495**

## Two systemic gaps worth fixing (this is why it reached main)

1. **`ci-e2e.yml` has no `pull_request` trigger** — it runs only on push to `main` or manual dispatch. So #488 was never E2E-tested before merge.
2. **The `desktop` path filter excludes `packages/sdk-core/**`** (it only watches `packages/crypto/src/**`). So even on push to main, SDK changes don't trigger Desktop E2E.

I'd suggest adding `packages/sdk-core/src/**`, `packages/sdk/src/**`, and `tests/desktop-e2e/**` to the desktop filter, and optionally a `pull_request` trigger. That's a CI-behavior change beyond the immediate bug, so I left it out of #495 — want me to do it as a follow-up?

The background watcher will report the cross-client-sync edit-step result across all platforms once the dispatched run finishes (~10 min).

## Status

- **Root cause:** #488 changed `updateFileMetadata` to publish the file record internally; the E2E helper `edit-filepointer.mjs` still used the old `ipnsRecord` contract → 400. (Not the phase-46 PR — that was docs-only.)
- **Fix:** PR **#495** — validated green on all platforms via the dispatched run. Ready to merge.

The only outstanding item is the optional CI-hardening follow-up I flagged (add `packages/sdk-core/**` + `tests/desktop-e2e/**` to the desktop path filter, and/or a `pull_request` trigger) so SDK contract drift gets caught before reaching `main`. Say the word and I'll open that as a separate PR.

FILE: packages/sdk-core/src/cas.ts:23
--- BODY ---
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Backoff jitter is one-sided, not ±50% as documented.**

The current multiplier (`0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5`) produces only `[-50%, 0%]` jitter. That biases retries earlier and weakens collision spreading under concurrent 409 retries.




<details>
<summary>Suggested fix</summary>

```diff
 function retryDelayMs(attempt: number): number {
-  return Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** attempt, BACKOFF_CAP_MS) * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5);
+  const base = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** attempt, BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
+  return base * (0.5 + Math.random()); // ±50% jitter => [0.5x, 1.5x)
 }
```
</details>
Based on learnings from `.planning/phases/47-sdk-folder-state-publish-consolidation/47-PATTERNS.md`, `retryDelayMs` is expected to use exponential backoff with ±50% jitter.

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against current code. Fix only still-valid issues, skip the
rest with a brief reason, keep changes minimal, and validate.

In `@packages/sdk-core/src/cas.ts` around lines 20 - 23, The jitter multiplier in
the retryDelayMs function is producing only -50% to 0% jitter (range 0.5 to 1.0)
instead of the documented ±50% jitter (range 0.5 to 1.5). Fix this by adjusting
the jitter multiplier calculation from (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5) to (0.5 +
Math.random()) so that the result properly spans from 0.5 (representing -50%
reduction) to 1.5 (representing +50% increase), ensuring symmetric jitter
distribution for better collision spreading under concurrent retries.
```

</details>

_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Key zeroization misses early failure paths before the `try/finally`.**

`resolveIpnsRecord(...)` and the immediate “not found” throw happen before the `try`. If either path fails, `fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0)` is skipped.




<details>
<summary>Suggested fix</summary>

```diff
-  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx);
-  if (!resolved) {
-    throw new Error(
-      `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
-    );
-  }
-
-  try {
+  try {
+    const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx);
+    if (!resolved) {
+      throw new Error(
+        `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
+      );
+    }
+
     const result = await publishWithCas<FileMetadata>({
       ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
       ipnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
       sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
       ctx: params.ctx,
       maxAttempts: 4,
       backoff: true,
       // ...
     });

     // ...
     return {
       ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
       metadataCid: result.cid,
       newSequenceNumber: result.newSequenceNumber,
       prunedCids: combinedPruned,
     };
   } finally {
     params.fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   }
```
</details>
As per coding guidelines, sensitive key material must be cleared from memory after use, and Phase 47 security explicitly requires caller-owned zeroing on all exit paths.
 


Also applies to: 368-372

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against current code. Fix only still-valid issues, skip the
rest with a brief reason, keep changes minimal, and validate.

In `@packages/sdk-core/src/file/index.ts` around lines 273 - 286, The
resolveIpnsRecord call and its "not found" error check are happening before the
try/finally block that zeros fileIpnsPrivateKey, creating a security gap where
the key isn't zeroed if either of those operations fail. Move the
resolveIpnsRecord call and the subsequent null check inside the try block so
that all exit paths, including early failures, will trigger the finally block to
execute fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0) and properly zero the sensitive key material.
```

225	export async function updateFileMetadata(params: {
226	  fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
227	  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
228	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
229	  currentMetadata: FileMetadata;
230	  updates: Partial<
231	    Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>
232	  >;
233	  createVersion: boolean;
234	  maxVersionsPerFile?: number;
235	  ctx: SdkContext;
236	}): Promise<{
237	  ipnsName: string;
238	  metadataCid: string;
239	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
240	  prunedCids: string[];
241	}> {
242	  const maxVersions = params.maxVersionsPerFile ?? MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE;
243	
244	  // 1. Build version history for the initial (pre-conflict) metadata
245	  let versions: VersionEntry[] | undefined;
246	  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
247	
248	  if (params.createVersion) {
249	    const versionEntry: VersionEntry = {
250	      cid: params.currentMetadata.cid,
251	      fileKeyEncrypted: params.currentMetadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
252	      fileIv: params.currentMetadata.fileIv,
253	      size: params.currentMetadata.size,
254	      timestamp: Date.now(),
255	      encryptionMode: params.currentMetadata.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
256	    };
257	    const allVersions = [versionEntry, ...(params.currentMetadata.versions ?? [])];
258	
259	    versions = allVersions.slice(0, maxVersions);
260	    prunedCids = allVersions.slice(maxVersions).map((v) => v.cid);
261	  } else {
262	    versions = params.currentMetadata.versions;
263	  }
264	
265	  // 2. Merge updates into current metadata
266	  const updatedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
267	    ...params.currentMetadata,
268	    ...params.updates,
269	    ...(versions && versions.length > 0 ? { versions } : { versions: undefined }),
270	    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
271	  };
272	
273	  // 3. Resolve the current IPNS record once up front to establish the CAS base
274	  //    sequence number (publishWithCas re-resolves authoritatively on each 409).
275	  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx);
276	  if (!resolved) {
277	    throw new Error(
278	      `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
279	    );
280	  }
281	
282	  // 4. Publish with CAS — on 409 apply latest-wins + loser-becomes-version (D-07).
283	  //    The publishWithCas engine owns the resolve→merge→retry skeleton; the
284	  //    domain-specific encode/decode/merge are injected as callbacks. The
285	  //    fileIpnsPrivateKey is zeroed in the finally on ALL exit paths (T-47-01).
286	  try {
287	    const result = await publishWithCas<FileMetadata>({
288	      ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
289	      ipnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
290	      sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
291	      ctx: params.ctx,
292	      maxAttempts: 4,
293	      backoff: true,
294	      encodeAndUpload: (metadata) => encryptAndUpload(metadata, params.folderKey, params.ctx),
295	      decodeRemote: (cid) => fetchAndDecryptFileMetadata(cid, params.folderKey, params.ctx),
296	      // Latest-wins three-way merge. `base` is unused for files (latest-wins, not
297	      // structural merge); `local` is the writer's metadata, `remote` is the
298	      // conflicting authoritative metadata.
299	      merge: (_base, local, remote) => {
300	        // Latest-wins by modifiedAt (>= prefers local on tie)
301	        const localModifiedAt = local.modifiedAt ?? 0;
302	        const remoteModifiedAt = remote.modifiedAt ?? 0;
303	        const localWins = localModifiedAt >= remoteModifiedAt;
304	
305	        const winner = localWins ? local : remote;
306	        const loser = localWins ? remote : local;
307	
308	        // The loser's current content becomes a VersionEntry
309	        const loserAsVersion: VersionEntry = {
310	          cid: loser.cid,
311	          fileKeyEncrypted: loser.fileKeyEncrypted,
312	          fileIv: loser.fileIv,
313	          size: loser.size,
314	          timestamp: loser.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
315	          encryptionMode: (loser.encryptionMode as 'GCM' | 'CTR') ?? 'GCM',
316	        };
317	
318	        // Merge: winner's versions + loserAsVersion merged with the loser's version
319	        // history. The second arg MUST be loser.versions, not remote.versions: when the
320	        // remote wins (localWins === false) the loser is the LOCAL metadata, so passing
321	        // remote.versions would silently drop the local writer's prior version history.
322	        const { versions: mergedVersions, prunedCids: extraPruned } = mergeVersions(
323	          [...(winner.versions ?? []), loserAsVersion],
324	          loser.versions,
325	          maxVersions
326	        );
327	
328	        const mergedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
329	          ...winner,
330	          versions: mergedVersions.length > 0 ? mergedVersions : undefined,
331	          modifiedAt: winner.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
332	        };
333	
334	        // CR-02 / D-07: a CID resurrected into mergedMetadata.versions by the remote
335	        // merge must NOT be returned for unpinning. Filter extraPruned against the set
336	        // of CIDs actually referenced by the published mergedMetadata. publishWithCas
337	        // de-dupes the accumulated prunedCids across attempts; this callback only filters
338	        // the CIDs it produces this round.
339	        const referenced = new Set([
340	          mergedMetadata.cid,
341	          ...(mergedMetadata.versions ?? []).map((v) => v.cid),
342	        ]);
343	        const filteredPruned = extraPruned.filter((c) => !referenced.has(c));
344	
345	        return { merged: mergedMetadata, prunedCids: filteredPruned };
346	      },
347	      localData: updatedMetadata,
348	    });
349	
350	    // Combine the pre-conflict prunedCids (from the initial version-history cap) with
351	    // any pruned during conflict merges, then strip CIDs the final published metadata
352	    // still references (CR-02): the initial prune may include a CID that a remote merge
353	    // resurrected into versions.
354	    const finalReferenced = new Set([
355	      result.publishedData.cid,
356	      ...(result.publishedData.versions ?? []).map((v) => v.cid),
357	    ]);
358	    const combinedPruned = [...new Set([...prunedCids, ...result.prunedCids])].filter(
359	      (c) => !finalReferenced.has(c)
360	    );
361	
362	    return {
363	      ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
364	      metadataCid: result.cid,
365	      newSequenceNumber: result.newSequenceNumber,
366	      prunedCids: combinedPruned,
367	    };
368	  } finally {
369	    // Zeroize the private key on all exit paths (T-47-01 / T-44-12). publishWithCas
370	    // never zeroes keys; the caller (this function) owns zeroing.
371	    params.fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
372	  }
373	}
374

376	 * Helper: encrypt FileMetadata with folderKey and upload to IPFS.
377	 * Returns the resulting CID.
378	 */
379	async function encryptAndUpload(
380	  metadata: FileMetadata,
381	  folderKey: Uint8Array,
382	  ctx: SdkContext
383	): Promise<string> {
384	  const encrypted = await encryptFileMetadata(metadata, folderKey);

With `createVersion: false`, pre-conflict pruning is empty, but a 409-conflict merge round *can* still produce `prunedCids` → orphaned pins. > AGENT

Finding 1 is **valid and confirms a real inconsistency**: the two sibling methods both capture and return `prunedCids` for the caller to unpin —

- `replaceFile` → `Promise<{ prunedCids }>`, captures + returns (lines 1172, 1224)
- `restoreFileVersion` → `Promise<{ prunedCids }>`, captures + returns (lines 1283, 1313)
- `deleteFileVersion` → `Promise<{ deletedCid }>`, **bare `await`, drops `prunedCids`** (line 1365)

Its own docstring says *"Same five-step shape as restoreFileVersion"*, yet it diverges. On a 409-conflict merge round, `updateFileMetadata` can prune versions → those CIDs leak as orphaned pins. Let me check the web caller and confirm findings 2 & 3 are genuine coverage gaps.

212	          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
213	        }
214	
215	        // Unpin deleted version CID
216	        unpinFromIpfs(deletedCid).catch((err) =>
217	          logger.warn('[Versions] Unpin deleted CID failed:', err)
218	        );
219	
220	        // Refresh quota
221	        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
222	
223	        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
224	      } catch (err) {
225	        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete version';
226	        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
227	        throw err;
228	      }
229	    },
230	    []
231	  );
232	
233	  return {
234	    ...state,
235	    restoreVersion: handleRestoreVersion,
236	    deleteVersion: handleDeleteVersion,
237	  };
238	}
239	
```

> AGENT

Verified all three. Here's the review result.

## CodeRabbit CLI review — phase 47 (`4b539b445..78e5f3d59`)

**3 findings — all valid.**

### 🟠 Major — pin leak in `deleteFileVersion` (real bug, in-scope for phase 47)
`packages/sdk/src/client.ts:1365` discards the `updateFileMetadata` return, dropping `prunedCids`. Its two sibling methods both capture + return them:
- `replaceFile` → `Promise<{ prunedCids }>` (1172, 1224)
- `restoreFileVersion` → `Promise<{ prunedCids }>` (1283, 1313)
- `deleteFileVersion` → `Promise<{ deletedCid }>` — drops them, despite its docstring claiming the *"same five-step shape as restoreFileVersion"*

The web caller `useFileVersions.ts:216` then unpins **only** `deletedCid`. On a 409-conflict merge round during version-delete, `updateFileMetadata` can prune versions → those CIDs become **orphaned pins** (quota leak). This is the same class as the `updateSharedFile` prunedCids leak that phase 47 already set out to fix.
**Fix (2 files):** capture `{ prunedCids }` in `deleteFileVersion`, return `{ deletedCid, prunedCids }`; unpin them in `handleDeleteVersion`.

### 🟡 Minor — two genuine test-coverage gaps in `cas.test.ts`
The new `publishWithCas` engine has 6 tests but misses:
- **resolve-returns-null-after-409** → should throw `ConflictError` without calling `decodeRemote`/`merge` (not covered by Test 2/3).
- **multi-round `prunedCids` accumulation + dedup** (Test 4 only covers single-round propagation).

Both are real paths in the engine; neither is a bug, just untested.

---

These weren't auto-applied since you asked to *run* the review and the major fix spans SDK + web. **My recommendation: fix the major pin leak now** (it's squarely a phase-47 goal) and **add the two `cas.test.ts` cases** while we're here. Want me to apply all three? Or just the major one?

37	 */
38	export async function publishWithCas<TData>(params: {
39	  ipnsName: string;
40	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
41	  ipnsPublicKey?: Uint8Array;
42	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
43	  ctx: SdkContext;
44	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
45	  keyEpoch?: number;
46	  maxAttempts: number;
47	  backoff: boolean;
48	  /** Encode local state to IPFS and return the resulting CID. */
49	  encodeAndUpload: (local: TData) => Promise<string>;
50	  /** Decode remote data from IPFS using the given CID. */
51	  decodeRemote: (cid: string) => Promise<TData>;
52	  /**
53	   * Three-way merge returning merged data and optional pruned CIDs.
54	   *
55	   * `base` is `undefined` when the caller omits `baseData` (e.g. the latest-wins
56	   * file path, which ignores `base`); merge implementations that read `base`
57	   * must defend against `undefined`.
58	   */
59	  merge: (
60	    base: TData | undefined,
61	    local: TData,
62	    remote: TData
63	  ) => { merged: TData; prunedCids?: string[] };
64	  /** Initial local data for the first publish attempt. */
65	  localData: TData;
66	  /** Base snapshot for three-way merge. */
67	  baseData?: TData;
68	}): Promise<{
69	  cid: string;
70	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
71	  publishedData: TData;
72	  prunedCids: string[];
73	}> {
74	  let currentSeq = params.sequenceNumber;
75	  let localData = params.localData;
76	  let lastRemoteSeq: bigint = params.sequenceNumber;
77	  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
78	
79	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < params.maxAttempts; attempt++) {
80	    // 1. Encode + upload (domain-specific, injected)
81	    const cid = await params.encodeAndUpload(localData);
82	    const newSeq = currentSeq + 1n;
83	
84	    try {
85	      // 2. CAS publish with expectedSequenceNumber guard
86	      await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
87	        ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
88	        ipnsPublicKey: params.ipnsPublicKey,
89	        ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
90	        metadataCid: cid,
91	        sequenceNumber: newSeq,
92	        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
93	        keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
94	        expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
95	        ctx: params.ctx,
96	      });
97	      return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq, publishedData: localData, prunedCids };
98	    } catch (err) {
99	      if (!is409(err)) throw err;
100	
101	      // 3. Re-resolve authoritatively — ignore seq hint in error body
102	      const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.ipnsName, params.ctx);
103	      if (!resolved) {
104	        throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, attempt + 1, lastRemoteSeq);
105	      }
106	      currentSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
107	      lastRemoteSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
108	
109	      // 4. Fetch + decode remote
110	      const remoteData = await params.decodeRemote(resolved.cid);
111	
112	      // 5. Three-way merge (domain-specific, injected)
113	      const { merged, prunedCids: extraPruned } = params.merge(
114	        params.baseData,
115	        localData,
116	        remoteData
117	      );
118	      localData = merged;
119	      prunedCids = [...new Set([...prunedCids, ...(extraPruned ?? [])])];
120	
121	      // 6. After the final attempt, throw ConflictError
122	      if (attempt === params.maxAttempts - 1) {
123	        throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, params.maxAttempts, lastRemoteSeq);
124	      }
125	
126	      // 7. Backoff + jitter before next attempt
127	      if (params.backoff) {
128	        await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, retryDelayMs(attempt)));
129	      }
130	    }
131	  }
132	
133	  // Unreachable — ConflictError is thrown inside the loop above on exhaustion
134	  throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, params.maxAttempts, lastRemoteSeq);
135	}
136

> AGENT

Key nuance confirmed: the restore handler unpins **web-computed** `prunedCids` (cap-pruning is deterministic there). But `deleteFileVersion` deletes a version (count decreases — no web-side cap-pruning), so its only `prunedCids` come from **409-conflict merge rounds**, and those are currently dropped at both layers. The fix must thread the SDK-returned `prunedCids` through to the web unpin. Let me check for existing tests asserting `deleteFileVersion`'s return shape before changing it.

> AGENT

Existing `deleteFileVersion` tests only assert `result.deletedCid` (not exact-equality), and they mock `updateFileMetadata` to return `prunedCids: []` — so adding `prunedCids` to the return is non-breaking. `computeDeleteVersionUpdate` returns no `prunedCids` (delete reduces count), confirming the SDK return is the only source. Implementing all changes now.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/client.ts",
  "old_string": "   * @param parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder containing the file\n   * @param fileId - ID of the file (FilePointer) whose version is deleted\n   * @param versionIndex - Index of the version being deleted (caller-resolved)\n   * @param params - Pre-resolved key + metadata + version-pruned updates + deletedCid\n   * @returns The deleted version's CID the caller should unpin\n   */\n  async deleteFileVersion(\n    parentIpnsName: string,\n    fileId: string,\n    versionIndex: number,\n    params: {\n      fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n      currentMetadata: FileMetadata;\n      updates: Partial<\n        Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>\n      >;\n      deletedCid?: string;\n      maxVersionsPerFile?: number;\n      /** Hex-encoded re-wrapped IPNS key to persist on the FilePointer (lazy migration). */\n      migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted?: string;\n    }\n  ): Promise<{ deletedCid?: string }> {\n    void versionIndex;\n    return this.withOperation('deleteFileVersion', async () => {\n      const folder = this.folderTree.get(parentIpnsName);\n      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');\n\n      const filePointer = folder.children.find(\n        (c): c is FilePointer => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileId\n      );\n      if (!filePointer) throw new Error('File not found');\n\n      // 1. Publish file metadata (CAS internally). updateFileMetadata zeroes the\n      //    fileIpnsPrivateKey in its own finally (T-47-01) — do NOT zero it here.\n      await sdkCore.updateFileMetadata({\n        fileIpnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,\n        fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,\n        folderKey: folder.folderKey,\n        currentMetadata: params.currentMetadata,\n        updates: params.updates,\n        createVersion: false,\n        maxVersionsPerFile: params.maxVersionsPerFile,\n        ctx: this.ctx,\n      });",
  "new_string": "   * @param parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder containing the file\n   * @param fileId - ID of the file (FilePointer) whose version is deleted\n   * @param versionIndex - Index of the version being deleted (caller-resolved)\n   * @param params - Pre-resolved key + metadata + version-pruned updates + deletedCid\n   * @returns The deleted version's `deletedCid` plus any `prunedCids` produced by a\n   *   409-conflict merge round inside `updateFileMetadata` — the caller must unpin both.\n   */\n  async deleteFileVersion(\n    parentIpnsName: string,\n    fileId: string,\n    versionIndex: number,\n    params: {\n      fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n      currentMetadata: FileMetadata;\n      updates: Partial<\n        Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>\n      >;\n      deletedCid?: string;\n      maxVersionsPerFile?: number;\n      /** Hex-encoded re-wrapped IPNS key to persist on the FilePointer (lazy migration). */\n      migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted?: string;\n    }\n  ): Promise<{ deletedCid?: string; prunedCids: string[] }> {\n    void versionIndex;\n    return this.withOperation('deleteFileVersion', async () => {\n      const folder = this.folderTree.get(parentIpnsName);\n      if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');\n\n      const filePointer = folder.children.find(\n        (c): c is FilePointer => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileId\n      );\n      if (!filePointer) throw new Error('File not found');\n\n      // 1. Publish file metadata (CAS internally). updateFileMetadata zeroes the\n      //    fileIpnsPrivateKey in its own finally (T-47-01) — do NOT zero it here.\n      //    Capture prunedCids: deleting a version never caps the history (count\n      //    decreases), but a 409-conflict merge round can re-add versions past the\n      //    cap, and those CIDs must be unpinned by the caller (matches replaceFile /\n      //    restoreFileVersion).\n      const { prunedCids } = await sdkCore.updateFileMetadata({\n        fileIpnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,\n        fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,\n        folderKey: folder.folderKey,\n        currentMetadata: params.currentMetadata,\n        updates: params.updates,\n        createVersion: false,\n        maxVersionsPerFile: params.maxVersionsPerFile,\n        ctx: this.ctx,\n      });"
}

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts",
  "old_string": "        try {\n          // client.deleteFileVersion owns the file IPNS publish (CAS internally), the\n          // conditional folder publish for lazy IPNS-key migration, folderTree\n          // bookkeeping, and the folder:updated emission. The store's\n          // children/sequenceNumber are written ONLY by the folder:updated subscription —\n          // no direct store writes here. fileIpnsPrivateKey is zeroed in this finally\n          // (T-47-01); sdk-core updateFileMetadata also zeroes its own copy.\n          await getSdkClient().deleteFileVersion(parentFolder.ipnsName, fileId, versionIndex, {\n            fileIpnsPrivateKey,\n            currentMetadata: prunedMetadata,\n            updates,\n            deletedCid,\n            maxVersionsPerFile: useVaultSettingsStore.getState().settings.maxVersionsPerFile,\n            ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted ? { migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } : {}),\n          });\n        } finally {\n          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);\n        }\n\n        // Unpin deleted version CID\n        unpinFromIpfs(deletedCid).catch((err) =>\n          logger.warn('[Versions] Unpin deleted CID failed:', err)\n        );",
  "new_string": "        let prunedCids: string[] = [];\n        try {\n          // client.deleteFileVersion owns the file IPNS publish (CAS internally), the\n          // conditional folder publish for lazy IPNS-key migration, folderTree\n          // bookkeeping, and the folder:updated emission. The store's\n          // children/sequenceNumber are written ONLY by the folder:updated subscription —\n          // no direct store writes here. fileIpnsPrivateKey is zeroed in this finally\n          // (T-47-01); sdk-core updateFileMetadata also zeroes its own copy.\n          ({ prunedCids } = await getSdkClient().deleteFileVersion(\n            parentFolder.ipnsName,\n            fileId,\n            versionIndex,\n            {\n              fileIpnsPrivateKey,\n              currentMetadata: prunedMetadata,\n              updates,\n              deletedCid,\n              maxVersionsPerFile: useVaultSettingsStore.getState().settings.maxVersionsPerFile,\n              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted ? { migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } : {}),\n            }\n          ));\n        } finally {\n          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);\n        }\n\n        // Unpin the explicitly deleted version CID.\n        unpinFromIpfs(deletedCid).catch((err) =>\n          logger.warn('[Versions] Unpin deleted CID failed:', err)\n        );\n\n        // Unpin any CIDs pruned by a 409-conflict merge inside the publish (distinct\n        // from deletedCid; only non-empty when a concurrent write forced a merge round).\n        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {\n          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch((err) =>\n            logger.warn('[Versions] Unpin pruned CID failed:', err)\n          );\n        }"
}

Pushed (`78e5f3d59..e9c288c5e`). All three CLI-review findings resolved and verified.

## Summary

Commit **`e9c288c5e`** on `feat/phase-47-sdk-folder-state-publish-consolidation` (PR #494), 4 files / +103 −13:

| Finding | Severity | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `deleteFileVersion` discards `prunedCids` → orphaned pins on 409-conflict merge | 🟠 Major | `deleteFileVersion` now returns `{ deletedCid, prunedCids }` (matching `replaceFile`/`restoreFileVersion`); the `useFileVersions` delete hook unpins them alongside `deletedCid` |
| Missing `publishWithCas` test: multi-round `prunedCids` accumulation + dedup | 🟡 Minor | Added `cas.test.ts` "Test 4b" |
| Missing `publishWithCas` test: 409 → null re-resolve → `ConflictError` (no decode/merge) | 🟡 Minor | Added `cas.test.ts` "Test 4c" + a `client-file-ops` test asserting `deleteFileVersion` surfaces `prunedCids` |

**Verification:** sdk-core 204 tests pass · sdk `client-file-ops` 9 pass · full root typecheck (all package builds + web `tsc -b`) exit 0. A 3-lens adversarial review of the diff returned **ship** on all lenses (pin-leak correctness, test validity, scope/regression).

## One follow-up worth tracking (out of scope here)

The adversarial review surfaced that **`restoreFileVersion`'s web hook has the same latent leak** — it unpins only the web-computed `prunedCids` and discards the SDK-returned ones, so a 409-conflict merge during *restore* leaks pins too. CodeRabbit flagged only `deleteFileVersion`, so I left it out of this PR. The SDK already returns `prunedCids` from `restoreFileVersion` (client.ts:1313); the fix is a one-line capture + unpin loop in the restore hook, mirroring what I just did for delete. Want me to fold that into this PR, or open it as a separate fix?

381	export function computeRestoreVersionUpdate(
382	  currentMetadata: FileMetadata,
383	  versionIndex: number
384	): { currentMetadata: FileMetadata; updates: FileContentUpdates; prunedCids: string[] } {
385	  const versions = currentMetadata.versions;
386	  if (!versions || versionIndex < 0 || versionIndex >= versions.length) {
387	    throw new Error('Invalid version index');
388	  }
389	
390	  const versionToRestore = versions[versionIndex];
391	
392	  // Build a version entry from CURRENT metadata (it becomes a past version)
393	  const currentAsVersion: VersionEntry = {
394	    cid: currentMetadata.cid,
395	    fileKeyEncrypted: currentMetadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
396	    fileIv: currentMetadata.fileIv,
397	    size: currentMetadata.size,
398	    timestamp: Date.now(),
399	    encryptionMode: currentMetadata.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
400	  };
401	
402	  // Remove restored version from array, prepend current as new version entry
403	  const remainingVersions = versions.filter((_, i) => i !== versionIndex);
404	  const newVersions = [currentAsVersion, ...remainingVersions];
405	
406	  // Prune if exceeds max
407	  const maxVer = getMaxVersionsPerFile();
408	  const retainedVersions = newVersions.slice(0, maxVer);
409	  const prunedCids = newVersions.slice(maxVer).map((v) => v.cid);
410	
411	  // currentMetadata carries the transformed versions; the client's updateFileMetadata
412	  // (createVersion: false) preserves these and overwrites the content fields via updates.
413	  return {
414	    currentMetadata: {
415	      ...currentMetadata,
416	      versions: retainedVersions.length > 0 ? retainedVersions : undefined,
417	    },
418	    updates: {
419	      cid: versionToRestore.cid,
420	      fileKeyEncrypted: versionToRestore.fileKeyEncrypted,
421	      fileIv: versionToRestore.fileIv,
422	      size: versionToRestore.size,
423	      encryptionMode: versionToRestore.encryptionMode,
424	    },
425	    prunedCids,
426	  };
427	}
428	
429	/**
430	 * Compute the metadata transform for deleting a past version (pure, no publish).
431	 *
432	 * Removes the version at `versionIndex` from the history. The current content is
433	 * untouched (no `updates`), so the client publishes the same content with a pruned
434	 * version list.
435	 *
436	 * @param currentMetadata - Current file metadata (must contain `versions`)
437	 * @param versionIndex - Index of version to delete
438	 * @returns Transformed currentMetadata, empty content updates, and the deleted CID
```

1. `useFileOperations.ts` `handleAddFile`/`handleAddFiles` — claimed **dead code** (active upload is `useDropUpload.ts` → `client.uploadFiles()`).
2. `useSharedWriteOps.ts` — uses `SharedWriteContext`, possibly **by-design** (shared folders aren't in the owner's `folderTree`).

> AGENT

Pushed (`e9c288c5e..026674a99`). Done — both requests resolved on PR #494.

## What I did

**1. Folded in the `restoreFileVersion` pin-leak fix** (the follow-up I flagged):
The restore hook discarded the SDK's returned `prunedCids` (conflict-merge pruning) and unpinned only web-computed cap-prune CIDs. Now it unpins the **deduped union** of both — same fix pattern as `deleteFileVersion`.

**2. Audited every mutative web path** for SDK-client routing (38 operations, 5 parallel lenses + targeted verification):

| Category | Count | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Routed through SDK client | 18 | ✅ folder CRUD, file replace, version restore/delete, all bin ops |
| Out of scope (non-folder) | 11 | ✅ share API calls, vault-init bootstrap, separate IPNS records (device-registry, vault-settings, BYO config) |
| Bypass — dead code | 2 | **Removed** |
| Bypass — by design | 5 | `useSharedWriteOps` (shared folders use a separate `SharedWriteContext`, not the owner's `folderTree`) — left as-is |

**Removed** `handleAddFile`/`handleAddFiles` from `useFileOperations` (−310 lines): they called `sdk-core` `addFileToFolder`/`addFilesToFolder` directly (old folderTree-bypassing pattern) but were verifiably never invoked — the live upload path is `getSdkClient().uploadFiles()` via `useDropUpload`. Also dropped the now-unused imports and the `useFolder` re-exports.

**Verification:** root typecheck EXIT 0 (all dep builds + web `tsc -b`) · sdk-core 204 tests · sdk `client-file-ops` 9 tests · eslint clean on the touched files.

## Two notes

- **`useSharedWriteOps` is the one place where folder-mutating writes don't go through the client** — by design today, because shared-with-me folders aren't tracked in the owner's `folderTree`. Unifying that would mean teaching the SDK client to manage shared-folder state, which is a real architectural change beyond phase 47's scope. Worth a backlog item if you want true single-ownership across shared folders too — say the word and I'll capture it.
- PR #494 now has three commits from this session (`78e5f3d59`, `e9c288c5e`, `026674a99`). CI on the branch is push-gated as usual.
